Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The solar_physics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 284 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 156 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on solar_physics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 284. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 156. QED.
